Signs in as the admin and reads\\r\\n * under normal admin RLS — the same visibility the Teacher Desk has.\\r\\n *\\r\\n *   npx vite-node scripts/student-work.ts -- --student \\"Celia\\"\\r\\n *   npx vite-node scripts/student-work.ts -- --student \\"Steven Morris\\" --days 30\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport fs from \\"node:fs\\";\\r\\nimport path from \\"node:path\\";\\r\\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \\"node:url\\";\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \\"@/integrations/supabase/client\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nconst REPO = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \\"..\\");\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction readEnv(file: string): Record<string, string> {\\r\\n  const out: Record<string, string> = {};\\r\\n  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return out;\\r\\n  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(file, \\"utf8\\").split(/\\\\r?\\\\n/)) {\\r\\n    const t = line.trim();\\r\\n    if (!t || t.startsWith(\\"#\\")) continue;\\r\\n    const i = t.indexOf(\\"=\\");\\r\\n    if (i < 0) continue;\\r\\n    out[t.slice(0, i).trim()] = t.slice(i + 1).trim().replace(/^[\\"\']|[\\"\']$/g, \\"\\");\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return out;\\r\\n}\\r\\nconst env = {\\r\\n  ...readEnv(path.resolve(REPO, \\"../../.env\\")),\\r\\n  ...readEnv(path.join(REPO, \\".env\\")),\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction arg(n: string) {\\r\\n  const i = process.argv.indexOf(`--${n}`);\\r\\n  return i >= 0 ? process.argv[i + 1] : undefined;\\r\\n}\\r\\nconst query = arg(\\"student\\");\\r\\nconst days = Number(arg(\\"days\\") ?? 30);\\r\\nif (!query) {\\r\\n  console.error(\'usage: vite-node scripts/student-work.ts -- --student <name|email> [--days 30]\');\\r\\n  process.exit(2);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst since = new Date(Date.now() - days * 86400_000).toISOString();\\r\\nconst d = (s: string | null) => (s ? s.slice(0, 10) : \\"—\\");\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function main() {\\r\\n  const { error: authErr } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({\\r\\n    email: env.LSATPREP_ADMIN_EMAIL,\\r\\n    password: [REDACTED],\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  if (authErr) {\\r\\n    console.error(`sign-in failed: ${authErr.message}`);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: users, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke(\\"admin-manage-users\\", {\\r\\n    method: \\"GET\\",\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  if (error) throw new Error(`admin-manage-users failed: ${error.message}`);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const q = query!.trim().toLowerCase();\\r\\n  const rows = (users ?? []) as { class_id: string; display_name: string | null; email: string | null }[];\\r\\n  const exact = rows.filter((r) => r.email?.toLowerCase() === q || r.display_name?.toLowerCase() === q);\\r\\n  const pool = exact.length\\r\\n    ? exact\\r\\n    : rows.filter((r) => r.display_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.email?.toLowerCase().includes(q));\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (pool.length !== 1) {\\r\\n    console.error(pool.length ? `\\"${query}\\" is ambiguous:` : `no student matches \\"${query}\\".`);\\r\\n    for (const r of pool) console.error(`  - ${r.display_name ?? \\"(no name)\\"} <${r.email ?? \\"?\\"}>`);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const s = pool[0];\\r\\n  const id = s.class_id;\\r\\n\\r\\n  console.log(`\\\\n${s.display_name ?? \\"(no name)\\"}  <${s.email ?? \\"?\\"}>`);\\r\\n  console.log(`last ${days} days\\\\n${\\"─\\".repeat(60)}`);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Activities\\r\\n  const { data: acts } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"activity_assignments\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"id,title,status,mode,created_at\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", id)\\r\\n    .order(\\"created_at\\", { ascending: false });\\r\\n  console.log(`\\\\nACTIVITIES (${acts?.length ?? 0})`);\\r\\n  for (const a of acts ?? []) {\\r\\n    console.log(`  ${d(a.created_at)}  ${String(a.status).padEnd(12)} ${a.title}`);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Read, Recall, Colour grades\\r\\n  // There is no carried_pct column: the coverage figure lives inside the\\r\\n  // `section` JSON as mean_coverage. Naming a column that does not exist makes\\r\\n  // PostgREST answer 400, which this client surfaces as data: null, which read\\r\\n  // for a week as \\"she has no graded runs\\". Hence the explicit error print.\\r\\n  const { data: grades, error: gradeErr } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"activity_section_grades\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"source_section,attempt_no,section,graded_at,created_at\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", id)\\r\\n    .order(\\"created_at\\", { ascending: false });\\r\\n  if (gradeErr) console.log(`\\\\nREAD, RECALL, COLOUR — could not read grades: ${gradeErr.message}`);\\r\\n  if (grades?.length) {\\r\\n    console.log(`\\\\nREAD, RECALL, COLOUR — graded runs (${grades.length})`);\\r\\n    for (const g of grades) {\\r\\n      const s = (g.section ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;\\r\\n      const cov = s.mean_coverage;\\r\\n      const pct = cov == null ? \\"  —\\" : `${String(Math.round(Number(cov))).padStart(3)}%`;\\r\\n      const done = s.complete === false ? \\"  (partial)\\" : \\"\\";\\r\\n      const mins = s.total_seconds == null ? \\"\\" : `  ${Math.round(Number(s.total_seconds) / 60)} min`;\\r\\n      console.log(`  ${d(g.graded_at ?? g.created_at)}  ${pct} coverage   ${g.source_section ?? \\"?\\"}  attempt ${g.attempt_no ?? \\"?\\"}${mins}${done}`);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Guided drills\\r\\n  // The title is not on the assignment row — it lives on guided_drills, and the\\r\\n  // date column is assigned_at, not created_at. Reading the wrong names here\\r\\n  // returns a silent zero, which reads as \\"she has been given nothing\\".\\r\\n  const { data: drills, error: drillErr } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"guided_drill_assignments\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"status,assigned_at,completed_at,guided_drills(title)\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", id)\\r\\n    .order(\\"assigned_at\\", { ascending: false })\\r\\n    .limit(15);\\r\\n  if (drillErr) {\\r\\n    console.log(`\\\\nGUIDED DRILLS — could not read: ${drillErr.message}`);\\r\\n  } else {\\r\\n    console.log(`\\\\nGUIDED DRILLS (${drills?.length ?? 0} most recent)`);\\r\\n    for (const g of drills ?? []) {\\r\\n      const title = (g as { guided_drills?: { title?: string } }).guided_drills?.title ?? \\"(untitled)\\";\\r\\n      console.log(`  ${d(g.assigned_at)}  ${String(g.status).padEnd(12)} ${title}`);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Question-bank work\\r\\n  const { count: attempts } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"attempts\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"*\\", { count: \\"exact\\", head: true })\\r\\n    .eq(\\"user_id\\", id)\\r\\n    .gte(\\"created_at\\", since);\\r\\n  const { count: correct } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"attempts\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"*\\", { count: \\"exact\\", head: true })\\r\\n    .eq(\\"user_id\\", id)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"is_correct\\", true)\\r\\n    .gte(\\"created_at\\", since);\\r\\n  const pct = attempts ? Math.round((100 * (correct ?? 0)) / attempts) : null;\\r\\n  console.log(\\r\\n    `\\\\nQUESTIONS (last ${days}d): ${attempts ?? 0} attempted` +\\r\\n      (pct === null ? \\"\\" : `, ${correct} correct — ${pct}%`),\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Latest messages both ways\\r\\n  const { data: convs } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"conversation_participants\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"conversation_id\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"user_id\\", id);\\r\\n  const cids = (convs ?? []).map((c) => c.conversation_id);\\r\\n  if (cids.length) {\\r\\n    const { data: msgs } = await supabase\\r\\n      .from(\\"messages\\")\\r\\n      .select(\\"body,sender_id,created_at\\")\\r\\n      .in(\\"conversation_id\\", cids)\\r\\n      .order(\\"created_at\\", { ascending: false })\\r\\n      .limit(4);\\r\\n    if (msgs?.length) {\\r\\n      console.log(`\\\\nLAST MESSAGES`);\\r\\n      for (const m of msgs.reverse()) {\\r\\n        const who = m.sender_id === id ? \\"her\\" : \\"you\\";\\r\\n        const line = String(m.body).replace(/\\\\s+/g, \\" \\").slice(0, 70);\\r\\n        console.log(`  ${d(m.created_at)}  ${who.padEnd(4)} ${line}`);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  console.log(\\"\\");\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nmain()\\r\\n  .then(() => process.exit(0))\\r\\n  .catch((e) => {\\r\\n    console.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : e);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Who has been assigned work they cannot open.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Run with vite-node so `@/` resolves:\\r\\n *   npx vite-node scripts/access-audit.ts\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A missing feature flag does not look like a locked door from the student\'s\\r\\n * side. Classroom\'s feed drops rows the student cannot open, so the work is\\r\\n * simply not there, and the direct link answers \\"Not open yet. This part of the\\r\\n * classroom isn\'t in your plan right now.\\"\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Ivory Johnson lost a day to that on 2026-09-08: three Read, Recall, Colour\\r\\n * sections assigned, `has_activity_access` off, one mail on the Monday saying\\r\\n * the portal was empty and another on the Tuesday with a screenshot of the\\r\\n * gate. Nothing in the product noticed, because nothing was looking.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * `scripts/assign.ts` now turns the flag on as it assigns, so this should stay\\r\\n * empty. It exists for the rows that predate that guard, and for the work that\\r\\n * arrives by other paths: the admin UI, a Lovable-side write, a hand-run SQL.\\r\\n * Read-only. It names what is wrong and never fixes it, because opening a\\r\\n * feature on a student\'s account is Joshua\'s call.\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport fs from \\"node:fs\\";\\r\\nimport path from \\"node:path\\";\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \\"@/integrations/supabase/client\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nconst env: Record<string, string> = {};\\r\\nfor (const f of [path.resolve(\\"../../.env\\"), path.resolve(\\".env\\")]) {\\r\\n  if (!fs.existsSync(f)) continue;\\r\\n  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(f, \\"utf8\\").split(/\\\\r?\\\\n/)) {\\r\\n    const i = line.indexOf(\\"=\\");\\r\\n    if (i > 0 && !line.startsWith(\\"#\\")) {\\r\\n      env[line.slice(0, i).trim()] = line.slice(i + 1).trim().replace(/^[\\"\']|[\\"\']$/g, \\"\\");\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nawait supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({\\r\\n  email: env.LSATPREP_ADMIN_EMAIL,\\r\\n  password: [REDACTED],\\r\\n});\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * What an Activity needs before a student can reach it.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Only Activities are checked here, because `activity_assignments` is the only\\r\\n * table this reads. Guided drills live in their own table behind\\r\\n * `has_drill_access` and are worth adding the day one goes missing the same\\r\\n * way; nothing is claimed about them today.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst ACTIVITY_FLAG = \\"has_activity_access\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nconst { data: assignments, error: aErr } = await supabase\\r\\n  .from(\\"activity_assignments\\")\\r\\n  .select(\\"student_id,title,status,mode,created_at\\");\\r\\nif (aErr) throw new Error(`could not read assignments: ${aErr.message}`);\\r\\n\\r\\nconst rows = (assignments ?? []) as Array<{\\r\\n  student_id: string;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  status: string;\\r\\n  mode: string | null;\\r\\n  created_at: string;\\r\\n}>;\\r\\n\\r\\nconst ids = [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.student_id))].filter(Boolean);\\r\\nconst { data: profiles, error: pErr } = await supabase\\r\\n  .from(\\"profiles\\")\\r\\n  .select(\\"class_id,display_name,has_activity_access,has_drill_access\\")\\r\\n  .in(\\"class_id\\", ids);\\r\\nif (pErr) throw new Error(`could not read profiles: ${pErr.message}`);\\r\\n\\r\\nconst byId = new Map(\\r\\n  ((profiles ?? []) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((p) => [p.class_id as string, p]),\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nlet blocked = 0;\\r\\nfor (const id of ids) {\\r\\n  const profile = byId.get(id);\\r\\n  const mine = rows.filter((r) => r.student_id === id);\\r\\n  const who = (profile?.display_name as string) ?? `(no profile for ${id.slice(0, 8)})`;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!profile) {\\r\\n    console.log(`MISSING PROFILE  ${who}  ${mine.length} assignment(s)`);\\r\\n    blocked++;\\r\\n    continue;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const flag = ACTIVITY_FLAG;\\r\\n  if (profile[flag] === false) {\\r\\n    blocked++;\\r\\n    const open = mine.filter((r) => r.status !== \\"completed\\");\\r\\n    console.log(\\r\\n      `BLOCKED  ${who.padEnd(24)} ${flag}=false  ${mine.length} assigned, ${open.length} still open`,\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    for (const r of open.slice(0, 5)) {\\r\\n      console.log(`         \\"${r.title}\\"  ${r.created_at?.slice(0, 10)}`);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconsole.log(\\r\\n  blocked \\"@/integrations/supabase/client\\";\\r\\nimport { librarySortIndex } from \\"../content\\";\\r\\nimport type {\\r\\n  Activity,\\r\\n  ActivityAssignment,\\r\\n  ActivityAssignmentSummary,\\r\\n  ActivityInstructions,\\r\\n  ActivityItem,\\r\\n  ActivityItemResponse,\\r\\n  ActivityStatus,\\r\\n  JudgeSource,\\r\\n  Verdict,\\r\\n} from \\"./types\\";\\r\\nimport type { RunScore } from \\"../progress\\";\\r\\nimport type { SectionGrade, SectionGradeStub } from \\"./types\\";\\r\\nimport type { Accommodation } from \\"../recall/section\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n// Thin data layer for the Activity Centre. The jsonb columns (instructions,\\r\\n// items) come back from PostgREST as `Json`; we cast them to the domain shapes\\r\\n// declared in ./types on read. Errors are always thrown, never swallowed.\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction hydrateAssignment(row: Record<string, unknown>): ActivityAssignment {\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    ...(row as unknown as ActivityAssignment),\\r\\n    instructions: (row.instructions ?? {}) as ActivityInstructions,\\r\\n    items: (row.items ?? []) as ActivityItem[],\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction hydrateActivity(row: Record<string, unknown>): Activity {\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    ...(row as unknown as Activity),\\r\\n    instructions: (row.instructions ?? {}) as ActivityInstructions,\\r\\n    items: (row.items ?? []) as ActivityItem[],\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The columns a card actually draws. `items` is deliberately absent: it is the\\r\\n * whole per-student snapshot, ~30KB for Conditional Rephrase, shipped once per\\r\\n * row so a card could print a number the database can count.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst SUMMARY_COLUMNS =\\r\\n  \\"id,activity_id,student_id,title,subtitle,status,score,score_numerator,score_judged,score_total,scoring_version,instructions,created_at,started_at,completed_at,mode,source_section\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Student: list my activity assignments. RLS scopes to student_id = auth.uid(). */\\r\\nexport async function listStudentAssignments(\\r\\n  studentId: string,\\r\\n): Promise<ActivityAssignmentSummary[]> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"activity_assignments\\")\\r\\n    .select(`${SUMMARY_COLUMNS},item_count`)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", studentId)\\r\\n    .order(\\"created_at\\", { ascending: false });\\r\\n  if (!error) return ((data ?? []) as unknown as Record<string, unknown>[]).map(toSummary);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // `item_count` arrives with its own migration. Until it is applied the\\r\\n  // narrowed select is rejected for naming a column that does not exist, and a\\r\\n  // list that renders is worth more than the bytes this saves.\\r\\n  if (!isUnknownColumn(error)) throw error;\\r\\n  const fallback = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"activity_assignments\\")\\r\\n    .select(`${SUMMARY_COLUMNS},items`)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", studentId)\\r\\n    .order(\\"created_at\\", { ascending: false });\\r\\n  if (fallback.error) throw fallback.error;\\r\\n  return ((fallback.data ?? []) as unknown as Record<string, unknown>[]).map(toSummary);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * A list row. `item_count` is a generated column, so the count arrives without\\r\\n * the questions; the fallback path counts a carried array instead, for a\\r\\n * database that has not been given the column yet.\\r\\n */\\r\\nfunction toSummary(row: Record<string, unknown>): ActivityAssignmentSummary {\\r\\n  const summary = {\\r\\n    ...(row as unknown as ActivityAssignmentSummary),\\r\\n    instructions: (row.instructions ?? {}) as ActivityInstructions,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  if (typeof summary.item_count === \\"number\\") return summary;\\r\\n  const items = row.items;\\r\\n  return { ...summary, item_count: Array.isArray(items) ? items.length : 0 };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Student: fetch one assignment I own. */\\r\\nexport async function getStudentAssignment(\\r\\n  assignmentId: string,\\r\\n  studentId: string,\\r\\n): Promise<ActivityAssignment | null> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"activity_assignments\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"*\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"id\\", assignmentId)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", studentId)\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  return data ? hydrateAssignment(data) : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Admin: read any assignment by id alone, whoever it was issued to.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * `getStudentAssignment` filters on `student_id`, which is right for a student\\r\\n * and wrong for Joshua: opening a link he just handed out told him the activity\\r\\n * belonged to a different account, when the whole reason he has the link is to\\r\\n * look at it. RLS is the gate here, not this filter -- \\"Admins read all\\r\\n * activity assignments\\" already exists, and a non-admin calling this gets the\\r\\n * same `null` the student-scoped read would have given them.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The caller is responsible for running what comes back read-only when it is\\r\\n * somebody else\'s row. Nothing here writes.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function getAssignmentById(assignmentId: string): Promise<ActivityAssignment | null> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"activity_assignments\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"*\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"id\\", assignmentId)\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  return data ? hydrateAssignment(data) : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Does this assignment exist, but belong to somebody else?\\r\\n *\\r\\n * `getStudentAssignment` filters on `student_id` as well as `id`, so an\\r\\n * assignment issued to a DIFFERENT account comes back as `null` and is\\r\\n * indistinguishable from an id that was never real. The runner rendered both as\\r\\n * \\"Activity not found.\\", which sent a reader hunting for a broken route or an\\r\\n * RLS bug when the actual answer was \\"you are signed in as the wrong account\\".\\r\\n * That happens constantly to anyone holding both an admin and a student login.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * This probes by id alone and lets RLS answer. An admin can read every\\r\\n * assignment, so a `true` here means the row is real and owned by someone else.\\r\\n * A student reads only their own, so they get `false` and keep the plain\\r\\n * not-found wording, which for them is the truth. It never returns the row or\\r\\n * anything in it, only whether one exists.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function assignmentBelongsToAnotherAccount(assignmentId: string): Promise<boolean> {\\r\\n  // Every failure resolves to false rather than rejecting. This only ever\\r\\n  // upgrades an error message, so a probe that cannot answer must leave the\\r\\n  // plain wording alone instead of taking the screen down with it, or telling\\r\\n  // a student to go hunting for an account that does not exist.\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n      .from(\\"activity_assignments\\")\\r\\n      .select(\\"id\\")\\r\\n      .eq(\\"id\\", assignmentId)\\r\\n      .maybeSingle();\\r\\n    if (error) return false;\\r\\n    return Boolean(data);\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    return false;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Student: mark an assignment in_progress on first open (assigned -> in_progress only). */\\r\\nexport async function markAssignmentStarted(assignmentId: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const { error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"activity_assignments\\")\\r\\n    .update({ status: \\"in_progress\\", started_at: new Date().toISOString() })\\r\\n    .eq(\\"id\\", assignmentId)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"status\\", \\"assigned\\");\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * PostgREST\'s two ways of saying \\"you named a column I do not have\\": the schema\\r\\n * cache miss on insert, and Postgres\'s own undefined_column. Either means the\\r\\n * telemetry migration has not been applied to this database yet.\\r\\n */\\r\\nfunction isUnknownColumn(error: { code?: string; message?: string } | null): boolean {\\r\\n  if (!error) return false;\\r\\n  return error.code === \\"PGRST204\\" || error.code === \\"42703\\";\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Student: write a verdict-bearing attempt to the append-only log.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The telemetry fields ride along on the same row: which judge decided the\\r\\n * verdict, what the world\'s structural classifier would have said, and how long\\r\\n * it took. They are measurement, never score, and they are strictly less\\r\\n * valuable than the attempt itself — so if the database has not been given\\r\\n * their columns yet, the attempt is written without them rather than lost.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function recordItemResponse(input: {\\r\\n  assignmentId: string;\\r\\n  studentId: string;\\r\\n  itemOrder: number;\\r\\n  responseText: string;\\r\\n"}}'}]

public.guided_drills (\\r\\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\\r\\n  slug text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\\r\\n  week integer NOT NULL,\\r\\n  order_in_week integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\\r\\n  title text NOT NULL,\\r\\n  category text,\\r\\n  estimated_minutes integer,\\r\\n  question_count integer,\\r\\n  difficulty_min integer,\\r\\n  difficulty_max integer,\\r\\n  question_types text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\',\\r\\n  question_features text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\',\\r\\n  reasoning_types text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\',\\r\\n  start_button_label text DEFAULT \'Start\',\\r\\n  instruction_card jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\'::jsonb,\\r\\n  bank_filter jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\'::jsonb,\\r\\n  source_file text,\\r\\n  is_published boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\\r\\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.guided_drills ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Admins manage guided drills\\"\\r\\nON public.guided_drills FOR ALL TO authenticated\\r\\nUSING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\\r\\nWITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE INDEX idx_guided_drills_week ON public.guided_drills(week, order_in_week);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TRIGGER trg_guided_drills_updated\\r\\nBEFORE UPDATE ON public.guided_drills\\r\\nFOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Assignments\\r\\nCREATE TABLE public.guided_drill_assignments (\\r\\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\\r\\n  guided_drill_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.guided_drills(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\\r\\n  student_id uuid NOT NULL,\\r\\n  class_id text NOT NULL,\\r\\n  assigned_by uuid NOT NULL,\\r\\n  assigned_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  scheduled_for date,\\r\\n  unlock_at timestamptz,\\r\\n  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'assigned\',\\r\\n  read_card_at timestamptz,\\r\\n  started_at timestamptz,\\r\\n  completed_at timestamptz,\\r\\n  attempt_session_id uuid,\\r\\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.guided_drill_assignments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Admins manage all assignments\\"\\r\\nON public.guided_drill_assignments FOR ALL TO authenticated\\r\\nUSING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\\r\\nWITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Students read own assignments\\"\\r\\nON public.guided_drill_assignments FOR SELECT TO authenticated\\r\\nUSING (student_id = auth.uid());\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Students update own assignments\\"\\r\\nON public.guided_drill_assignments FOR UPDATE TO authenticated\\r\\nUSING (student_id = auth.uid())\\r\\nWITH CHECK (student_id = auth.uid());\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE INDEX idx_gda_student ON public.guided_drill_assignments(student_id, scheduled_for);\\r\\nCREATE INDEX idx_gda_drill ON public.guided_drill_assignments(guided_drill_id);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TRIGGER trg_gda_updated\\r\\nBEFORE UPDATE ON public.guided_drill_assignments\\r\\nFOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();\\r\\nGRANT INSERT, UPDATE, SELECT ON public.guided_drills TO sandbox_exec;\\r\\nGRANT INSERT, UPDATE, SELECT ON public.guided_drill_assignments TO sandbox_exec;\\r\\n-- Activity Centre Phase 1: per-student snapshotted assignments.\\r\\n-- Created at assign time by admins with title/subtitle/instructions/items\\r\\n-- snapshotted from the parent activities row. Later edits to the activity do\\r\\n-- not mutate already-issued assignments. If the activity is deleted, the\\r\\n-- assignment keeps its snapshot (activity_id goes NULL) so the student can\\r\\n-- still work it. Mirrors homework_assignments exactly.\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.activity_assignments (\\r\\n  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\\r\\n  activity_id   uuid REFERENCES public.activities(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\\r\\n  student_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\\r\\n  title         text NOT NULL,\\r\\n  subtitle      text,\\r\\n  instructions  jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\'::jsonb,\\r\\n  item_format   text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'free-text\',\\r\\n  items         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT \'[]\'::jsonb,\\r\\n  status        text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'assigned\'\\r\\n                CHECK (status IN (\'assigned\', \'in_progress\', \'completed\')),\\r\\n  score         numeric CHECK (score IS NULL OR (score >= 0 AND score <= 100)),\\r\\n  started_at    timestamptz,\\r\\n  completed_at  timestamptz,\\r\\n  assigned_by   uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id),\\r\\n  class_id      uuid NOT NULL,\\r\\n  created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.activity_assignments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Students: read + update own rows. Snapshot immutability enforced by trigger.\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Students read own activity assignments\\"\\r\\n  ON public.activity_assignments FOR SELECT TO authenticated\\r\\n  USING (student_id = auth.uid());\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Students update own activity assignments\\"\\r\\n  ON public.activity_assignments FOR UPDATE TO authenticated\\r\\n  USING (student_id = auth.uid())\\r\\n  WITH CHECK (student_id = auth.uid());\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Admin full CRUD.\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Admins read all activity assignments\\"\\r\\n  ON public.activity_assignments FOR SELECT TO authenticated\\r\\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Admins insert activity assignments\\"\\r\\n  ON public.activity_assignments FOR INSERT TO authenticated\\r\\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') AND assigned_by = auth.uid());\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Admins update activity assignments\\"\\r\\n  ON public.activity_assignments FOR UPDATE TO authenticated\\r\\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\\r\\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Admins delete activity assignments\\"\\r\\n  ON public.activity_assignments FOR DELETE TO authenticated\\r\\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Immutability guard: students cannot mutate snapshot or attribution columns.\\r\\n-- Allowed student writes: status, score, started_at, completed_at.\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_activity_assignment_immutables()\\r\\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  IF public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN\\r\\n    RETURN NEW;\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  IF NEW.activity_id   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.activity_id   OR\\r\\n     NEW.student_id    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.student_id    OR\\r\\n     NEW.title         IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.title         OR\\r\\n     NEW.subtitle      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.subtitle      OR\\r\\n     NEW.instructions  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.instructions  OR\\r\\n     NEW.item_format   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.item_format   OR\\r\\n     NEW.items         IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.items         OR\\r\\n     NEW.assigned_by   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.assigned_by   OR\\r\\n     NEW.class_id      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.class_id      OR\\r\\n     NEW.created_at    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\\r\\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'activity_assignments: snapshot columns are immutable for non-admin users\';\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  RETURN NEW;\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\n\\r\\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS activity_assignments_guard_immutables ON public.activity_assignments;\\r\\nCREATE TRIGGER activity_assignments_guard_immutables\\r\\n  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.activity_assignments\\r\\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.guard_activity_assignment_immutables();\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Index for student Classroom queries: scan by student, filter by status.\\r\\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS activity_assignments_student_status_idx\\r\\n  ON public.activity_assignments (student_id, status, created_at DESC);\\r\\n\\r\\n-- At most one active assignment per (student, activity). A completed assignment\\r\\n-- does not block re-issuing the same activity.\\r\\nCREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS activity_assignments_active_unique\\r\\n  ON public.activity_assignments (student_id, activity_id)\\r\\n  WHERE status <> \'completed\' AND activity_id IS NOT NULL;\\r\\n-- Activity Centre Phase 1: append-only per-item attempts.\\r\\n-- One row per submit. Drives the activity score and future analytics. The\\r\\n-- Coach verdict is recorded alongside the student\'s response and attempt\\r\\n-- number. Mirrors the abstract_translator_attempts append-only pattern:\\r\\n-- owner can read + insert their rows; admins can read all.\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.activity_item_responses (\\r\\n  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\\r\\n  assignment_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.activity_assignments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\\r\\n  student_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\\r\\n  item_order    integer NOT NULL,\\r\\n  response_text text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'\',\\r\\n  verdict       text CHECK (verdict IN (\'correct\', \'partial\', \'incorrect\')),\\r\\n  attempt_no    integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\\r\\n  created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS activity_item_responses_assignment_idx\\r\\n  ON public.activity_item_responses (assignment_id, item_order, created_at);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS activity_item_responses_student_idx\\r\\n  ON public.activity_item_responses (student_id, created_at DESC);\\r\\n\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.activity_item_responses ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"activity_item_responses: owner can select\\"\\r\\n  ON public.activity_item_responses FOR SELECT TO authenticated\\r\\n  USING (auth.uid() = student_id OR public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY SectionBriefing } from \'@/lib/sectionBriefing\';\\r\\nimport { singleSectionOf } from \'@/lib/drillRunSpec\';\\r\\nimport { sectionDirectionsRoute } from \'@/lib/sectionDirections\';\\r\\nimport { listAssignmentsForStudent as listGuidedForStudent, type GuidedDrill, type GuidedDrillAssignment } from \'@/lib/guidedDrills\';\\r\\nimport { listStudentAssignments as listActivityForStudent } from \'@/features/activity-centre/api/activitiesClient\';\\r\\nimport { activityWorkStatus, guidedWorkStatus, homeworkWorkStatus, studentWorkKey, teacherWorkStatus } from \'@/lib/studentWorkProjection\';\\r\\nimport { loadActivityWorkEvidence, loadAssignmentWorkEvidence, type GuidedWorkEvidence, type HomeworkWorkEvidence } from \'@/lib/studentWorkEvidence\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * A unified read-only \\"assigned\\" row surfaced in the student\'s Today list.\\r\\n * Status is derived from the underlying source row so completing work on the\\r\\n * assignment page reflects here without any extra plumbing.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport interface AssignedTodoRow {\\r\\n  key: string;\\r\\n  kind: \'homework\' | \'ta\' | \'activity\' | \'guided\' | \'bootcamp\' | \'plan\';\\r\\n  refId: string;\\r\\n  /** The table `refId` points into, so a classroom_items row can be matched to it. */\\r\\n  sourceTable: string;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  subtitle: string;\\r\\n  status: \'assigned\' | \'in_progress\' | \'completed\';\\r\\n  createdAt: string;\\r\\n  /** Due date, when a classroom_items row carries one. */\\r\\n  dueAt: string | null;\\r\\n  route: string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Work that reports its own completion, so the student is never asked to\\r\\n   * assert it. A timed section is the case: sitting it IS finishing it, and a\\r\\n   * \\"Mark done\\" button beside one can be pressed without doing the section at\\r\\n   * all, which is worse than useless on work assigned as a measurement.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  selfCompleting?: boolean;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Present only on a timed section. Home opens these instructions over the\\r\\n   * stage instead of navigating, so declining costs the student nothing.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  briefing?: SectionBriefing;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ntype GuidedRow = GuidedDrillAssignment & { guided_drill: GuidedDrill };\\r\\nconst EMPTY_ACTIVITY_EVIDENCE = new Set<string>();\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction normalizeStatus(raw: string | null | undefined): AssignedTodoRow[\'status\'] {\\r\\n  if (raw === \'completed\') return \'completed\';\\r\\n  if (raw === \'assigned\') return \'assigned\';\\r\\n  return \'in_progress\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** classroom_items types that represent work rather than reading material. */\\r\\nconst WORK_ITEM_KIND: Partial<Record<ClassroomItem[\'type\'], AssignedTodoRow[\'kind\']>> = {\\r\\n  drill: \'guided\',\\r\\n  homework: \'homework\',\\r\\n  activity: \'activity\',\\r\\n  bootcamp_module: \'bootcamp\',\\r\\n  weekly_plan_task: \'plan\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * `/guided/:id` reads `returnTo` to decide where finishing the drill lands the\\r\\n * student. Building it into the row would bake in whichever surface happened to\\r\\n * construct the feed, which is how every guided drill ended up returning to\\r\\n * `/schedule` no matter where it was launched from. Consumers call this at\\r\\n * click time with their own location instead.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function routeWithReturnTo(route: string, from: string): string {\\r\\n  if (!route.startsWith(\'/guided/\')) return route;\\r\\n  const [path, query = \'\'] = route.split(\'?\');\\r\\n  const params = new URLSearchParams(query);\\r\\n  params.set(\'returnTo\', from);\\r\\n  return `${path}?${params.toString()}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The single source for \\"what has been assigned to me\\".\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Two independent builders used to answer this question — this hook, and\\r\\n * `buildWorkRows` in classroomData.ts — reading overlapping but different\\r\\n * tables. The result was not merely inconsistent, it was disjoint: Home listed\\r\\n * guided drills and no bootcamp, the Classroom\'s Assigned work panel listed the\\r\\n * bootcamp and no drills. Everything now merges here.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Identity is `sourceTable:refId`. A classroom_items row that points at a\\r\\n * source assignment is the preferred representation of it — the teacher can\\r\\n * retitle it and set a due date — so it overrides, and `resolveItemRoute` sends\\r\\n * it to the same destination the raw row would have used.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport interface AssignedFeedSources {\\r\\n  hw: ReturnType<typeof useStudentAssignments>[\'assignments\'];\\r\\n  ta: ReturnType<typeof useStudentTAAssignments>[\'assignments\'];\\r\\n  guided: GuidedRow[];\\r\\n  activities: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listActivityForStudent>>;\\r\\n  items: ClassroomItem[];\\r\\n  guidedEvidence?: Record<string, GuidedWorkEvidence>;\\r\\n  homeworkEvidence?: Record<string, HomeworkWorkEvidence>;\\r\\n  activityEvidence?: ReadonlySet<string>;\\r\\n  /** Undefined until the source read succeeds; plan mirrors cannot stand in for it. */\\r\\n  weeklyPlanStatusById?: Readonly<Record<string, WeeklyPlanItem[\'status\']>>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The pure merge behind the hook, exported so the disjoint-feed and\\r\\n * duplicate-assignment rules can be tested without standing up five queries.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function buildAssignedRows({\\r\\n  hw,\\r\\n  ta,\\r\\n  guided,\\r\\n  activities,\\r\\n  items,\\r\\n  guidedEvidence = {},\\r\\n  homeworkEvidence = {},\\r\\n  activityEvidence = EMPTY_ACTIVITY_EVIDENCE,\\r\\n  weeklyPlanStatusById,\\r\\n}: AssignedFeedSources): AssignedTodoRow[] {\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    const byIdentity = new Map<string, AssignedTodoRow>();\\r\\n    const put = (row: AssignedTodoRow) => byIdentity.set(studentWorkKey({ sourceTable: row.sourceTable, assignmentId: row.refId }), row);\\r\\n\\r\\n    for (const a of hw) {\\r\\n      // A set that is exactly one PrepTest section is sat, not worked through:\\r\\n      // it opens on the section\'s directions page and the clock does the\\r\\n      // rest. Finishing the run completes the row, so it must never carry a\\r\\n      // \\"Mark done\\" the student could press without sitting it.\\r\\n      const section = singleSectionOf(a.question_qids ?? []);\\r\\n      put({\\r\\n        key: `hw-${a.id}`,\\r\\n        kind: \'homework\',\\r\\n        refId: a.id,\\r\\n        sourceTable: \'homework_assignments\',\\r\\n        title: a.set_title || \'Homework\',\\r\\n        subtitle: `${a.question_qids?.length ?? 0} questions${a.score != null ? ` · ${Math.round(a.score)}%` : \'\'}`,\\r\\n        status: homeworkWorkStatus(a, homeworkEvidence[a.id]),\\r\\n        createdAt: a.created_at,\\r\\n        dueAt: null,\\r\\n        route: section ? sectionDirectionsRoute(a.id) : `/classroom/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\\r\\n        selfCompleting: !!section,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    for (const a of ta) {\\r\\n      put({\\r\\n        key: `ta-${a.id}`,\\r\\n        kind: \'ta\',\\r\\n        refId: a.id,\\r\\n        sourceTable: \'ta_assignments\',\\r\\n        title: a.title || \'From Joshua\',\\r\\n        subtitle: a.pdf_url ? \'PDF attached\' : \'TA assignment\',\\r\\n        status: teacherWorkStatus(a),\\r\\n        createdAt: a.assigned_at,\\r\\n        dueAt: null,\\r\\n        route: `/classroom/ta/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Preserve every assignment instance, even when the teacher issued the\\r\\n    // same drill twice. The assignment ID, not guided_drill_id, is identity.\\r\\n    for (const g of guided) {\\r\\n      put({\\r\\n        key: `g-${g.id}`,\\r\\n        kind: \'guided\',\\r\\n        refId: g.id,\\r\\n        sourceTable: \'guided_drill_assignments\',\\r\\n        title: g.guided_drill?.title || \'Guided drill\',\\r\\n        subtitle: `${g.guided_drill?.question_count ?? \'?\'} questions${g.guided_drill?.estimated_minutes ? ` · ${g.guided_drill.estimated_minutes} min` : \'\'}`,\\r\\n        status: guidedWorkStatus(g, guidedEvidence[g.id]),\\r\\n        createdAt: g.assigned_at,\\r\\n        dueAt: null,\\r\\n        route: `/guided/${encodeURIComponent(g.id)}`,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    for (const a of activities) {\\r\\n      put({\\r\\n        key: `act-${a.id}`,\\r\\n        kind: \'activity\',\\r\\n        refId: a.id,\\r\\n        sourceTable: \'activity_assignments\',\\r\\n        title: a.title || \'Activity\',\\r\\n        subtitle: a.subtitle || \'Activity\',\\r\\n        status: activityWorkStatus(a, activityEvidence.has(a.id)),\\r\\n        createdAt: a.created_at,\\r\\n        dueAt: null,\\r\\n        route: `/activities/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    for (const item of items) {\\r\\n      const kind = WORK_ITEM_KIND[item.type];\\r\\n      if (!kind) continue; // notes and recaps are reading material, not work\\r\\n      // The plan item can be checked off independently of its classroom mirror.\\r\\n      // A missing source (including a failed or pending read) cannot certify a\\r\\n      // status, and a skipped task no longer belongs on an assigned-work list.\\r\\n      const planStatus = kind === \'plan\' && item.source_table === \'weekly_plan_items\' && item.source_id\\r\\n        ? weeklyPlanStatusById?.[item.source_id]\\r\\n        : undefined;\\r\\n      if (kind === \'plan\' && (!planStatus || planStatus === \'skipped\')) continue;\\r\\n      const route = resolveItemRoute(item);\\r\\n      if (!route) continue;\\r\\n      const sourceTable = item.source_table ?? \'classroom_items\';\\r\\n      const refId = item.source_id ?? item.id;\\r\\n      const identity = studentWorkKey({ sourceTable, assignmentId: refId });\\r\\n      const existing = byIdentity.get(identity);\\r\\n      // Completion wins over the mirror. A student who finished the underlying\\r\\n      // assignment must not see it come back because the classroom_items row it\\r\\n      // was mirrored into still says \\"assigned\\" — that is why finished work kept\\r\\n      // reappearing on Home.\\r\\n      const itemStatus = normalizeStatus(item.status);\\r\\n      // Guided and written teacher assignments have their own persisted\\r\\n      // completion/progress evidence. A stale or manually updated mirror must\\r\\n      // not promote them merely because the page was opened or a button was\\r\\n      // pressed outside the actual work flow.\\r\\n      const sourceOwnsStatus = existing && (\\r\\n        sourceTable === \'guided_drill_assignments\' ||\\r\\n        sourceTable === \'homework_assignments\' ||\\r\\n        sourceTable === \'ta_assignments\' ||\\r\\n        sourceTable === \'activity_assignments\'\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      const status = kind === \'plan\' && planStatus && planStatus !== \'skipped\'\\r\\n        ? planStatus\\r\\n        : sourceOwnsStatus\\r\\n        ? existing.status\\r\\n        : itemStatus === \'completed\' || existing?.status === \'completed\' ? \'completed\' : itemStatus;\\r\\n      byIdentity.set(identity, {\\r\\n        key: `ci-${item.id}`,\\r\\n        kind,\\r\\n        refId,\\r\\n        sourceTable,\\r\\n        title: item.title || existing?.title || \'Assigned work\',\\r\\n        // The item carries no question count, so keep the source row\'s subtitle\\r\\n        // when there is one.\\r\\n        subtitle: existing?.subtitle ?? item.description ?? \'\',\\r\\n        status,\\r\\n        createdAt: item.created_at,\\r\\n        dueAt: item.due_at,\\r\\n        // The mirror row was written before the section rule existed, so the\\r\\n        // source row\'s directions route wins over the item\'s classroom link.\\r\\n        route: existing?.selfCompleting ? existing.route : route,\\r\\n        selfCompleting:\\r\\n          (item.metadata_json as { kind?: string } | null)?.kind === \'timed_section\' ||\\r\\n          !!existing?.selfCompleting,\\r\\n        briefing: readSectionBriefing(item.metadata_json) ?? undefined,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Open work first, then completed; newest within each bucket.\\r\\n    return [...byIdentity.values()].sort((x, y) => {\\r\\n      const s = (x.status === \'completed\' ? 1 : 0) - (y.status === \'completed\' ? 1 : 0);\\r\\n      if (s !== 0) return s;\\r\\n      return new Date(y.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(x.createdAt).getTime();\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** @see buildAssignedRows — this hook only supplies it with live queries. */\\r\\nexport function useAssignedTodoFeed(): {\\r\\n  rows: AssignedTodoRow[];\\r\\n  loading: boolean;\\r\\n  /** A read failed. Never report \\"nothing assigned\\" on a failed read. */\\r\\n"}}'}]

That is correct for students and\\r\\n-- it is why the teacher workspace could not show a student\'s sittings — the\\r\\n-- admin account\'s queries came back empty rather than denied, which reads as\\r\\n-- \\"this student has done nothing\\" and is the worst possible failure mode.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- This adds ONE more way to read each table: being an admin. Nothing else\\r\\n-- changes. In particular:\\r\\n--   * No INSERT, UPDATE or DELETE policy is touched. An admin can read a\\r\\n--     student\'s work and cannot write, edit, or delete it — a teacher\'s view\\r\\n--     of a sitting must not be able to alter the sitting.\\r\\n--   * Admin is `public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\')`, the security-definer\\r\\n--     function already used for role checks, not a profile column a student\\r\\n--     could ever hold.\\r\\n--   * Students gain nothing. Their own policies are untouched and still the\\r\\n--     only way a non-admin reads anything.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- Tables are guarded with to_regclass so this migration is safe against an\\r\\n-- environment that does not have all of them.\\r\\n\\r\\ndo $$\\r\\ndeclare\\r\\n  t text;\\r\\n  tables text[] := array[\\r\\n    \'section_history\',        -- every section sat, with its per-question record\\r\\n    \'attempts\',               -- every question answered, anywhere on the platform\\r\\n    \'wrong_answer_journal\',   -- every miss, with the choice made\\r\\n    \'rc_attempts\',            -- reading comprehension, same shape\\r\\n    \'rc_drill_sessions\',\\r\\n    \'daily_stats\',            -- usage: questions and time spent, per day\\r\\n    \'question_usage\',\\r\\n    \'question_views\',\\r\\n    \'practice_test_runs\',     -- full practice tests and their legs\\r\\n    \'bootcamp_kv\'             -- bootcamp progress, guided drills, resume rows\\r\\n  ];\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  foreach t in array tables loop\\r\\n    if to_regclass(\'public.\' || t) is null then\\r\\n      raise notice \'skipping %, not present in this database\', t;\\r\\n      continue;\\r\\n    end if;\\r\\n\\r\\n    execute format(\\r\\n      \'drop policy if exists %I on public.%I\',\\r\\n      \'admins_read_\' || t, t\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    execute format(\\r\\n      \'create policy %I on public.%I for select to authenticated using (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'\'admin\'\'))\',\\r\\n      \'admins_read_\' || t, t\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  end loop;\\r\\nend $$;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Verification. ## assistant

Implemented guided assignment evidence from durable `drill_runs`, preserving legacy progress.

Changed:

- `src/lib/studentWorkEvidence.ts`: reads and paginates both namespaces under the selected student; validates owners, IDs, and assignment references; excludes empty/direct-launch sittings; preserves completion through retakes.
- `src/lib/drillRunLedger.ts`: exports the existing pure owned-row parser. Write behavior unchanged.
- `src/lib/studentWorkEvidence.test.ts`: covers malformed ownership, repeat assignments, migration precedence, deterministic selection, source failures, and pagination.

Focused tests passed **32/32**. Final evidence-file rerun passed **13/13**. No build, typecheck, publication, documentation changes, or live records created.

Remaining #166 gaps:

- `HubAssignments.tsx:132-139` deletes only an Activity classroom mirror. The owned source assignment remains and can reappear. Its displayed status also comes from the mirror rather than Activity evidence.
- Live student-to-teacher guided and Activity reconciliation still needs genuine account checks. Existing scripts support real assignment/read operations, but no authenticated student fixture harness was found.
- RLS integration and mutation attribution remain unverified. Existing admin reads are defined in `20260829220000_admin_reads_student_work.sql`.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"0d508e","wall_time_seconds":0.2919758,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":3405,"output":"import type { GuidedWorkEvidence, HomeworkWorkEvidence } from \'./studentWorkEvidence\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * An assignment is an issued instance of work, not the content it points to.\\r\\n * The same drill or activity can be assigned more than once, and each issue\\r\\n * must keep its own status and place in the student\'s history.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport type StudentWorkSource =\\r\\n  | \'homework_assignments\'\\r\\n  | \'ta_assignments\'\\r\\n  | \'guided_drill_assignments\'\\r\\n  | \'activity_assignments\'\\r\\n  | \'classroom_items\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type StudentWorkStatus = \'assigned\' | \'in_progress\' | \'completed\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface StudentWorkIdentity {\\r\\n  sourceTable: StudentWorkSource | string;\\r\\n  /** Primary key of the issued assignment row, never a drill or activity ID. */\\r\\n  assignmentId: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function studentWorkKey({ sourceTable, assignmentId }: StudentWorkIdentity): string {\\r\\n  return `${sourceTable}:${assignmentId}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Viewing instructions or an assignment does not start work. */\\r\\nexport function guidedWorkStatus(row: {\\r\\n  status: \'assigned\' | \'read\' | \'started\' | \'completed\';\\r\\n  completed_at: string | null;\\r\\n}, saved?: GuidedWorkEvidence): StudentWorkStatus {\\r\\n  if (row.status === \'completed\' && row.completed_at) return \'completed\';\\r\\n  if (saved?.savedAnswerCount && saved.status === \'completed\') return \'completed\';\\r\\n  if (saved?.savedAnswerCount) return \'in_progress\';\\r\\n  return \'assigned\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Homework is opened on first render; only a saved answer begins it. */\\r\\nexport function homeworkWorkStatus(row: {\\r\\n  status: \'assigned\' | \'in_progress\' | \'completed\';\\r\\n  completed_at: string | null;\\r\\n}, saved?: HomeworkWorkEvidence): StudentWorkStatus {\\r\\n  if (row.status === \'completed\' && row.completed_at) return \'completed\';\\r\\n  if (saved?.savedAnswerCount && saved.status === \'completed\') return \'completed\';\\r\\n  if (saved?.savedAnswerCount) return \'in_progress\';\\r\\n  return \'assigned\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** A saved response starts teacher-assigned written work; opening it does not. */\\r\\nexport function teacherWorkStatus(row: {\\r\\n  status: \'assigned\' | \'viewed\' | \'completed\';\\r\\n  response_saved_at?: string | null;\\r\\n  completed_at: string | null;\\r\\n}): StudentWorkStatus {\\r\\n  if (row.status === \'completed\' && row.completed_at) return \'completed\';\\r\\n  if (row.response_saved_at) return \'in_progress\';\\r\\n  return \'assigned\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** An Activity starts when a response is persisted, not when its page opens. */\\r\\nexport function activityWorkStatus(row: {\\r\\n  status: \'assigned\' | \'in_progress\' | \'completed\' | \'abandoned\';\\r\\n  completed_at: string | null;\\r\\n}, hasSavedResponse: boolean): StudentWorkStatus {\\r\\n  if (row.status === \'completed\' && row.completed_at) return \'completed\';\\r\\n  return hasSavedResponse ? \'in_progress\' : \'assigned\';\\r\\n}\\r\\nimport { describe, it, expect } from \'vitest\';\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  buildAssignedRows,\\r\\n  routeWithReturnTo,\\r\\n  type AssignedFeedSources,\\r\\n} from \'./useAssignedTodoFeed\';\\r\\nimport type { ClassroomItem } from \'@/hooks/useClassroomItems\';\\r\\n\\r\\n// Regression cover for the audit\'s H8/H9/H10. {\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    id: \'ga-1\',\\r\\n    guided_drill_id: \'drill-1\',\\r\\n    status: \'assigned\',\\r\\n    assigned_at: \'2026-05-22T15:31:33Z\',\\r\\n    guided_drill: { title: \'First [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"545480","wall_time_seconds":0.2327585,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":509,"output":" *\\r\\n * A missing `source_table` still counts: rows predating the column were written\\r\\n * by the assignment path, and nothing else has ever left it null with an id.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst ASSIGNMENT_SOURCE_TABLES = new Set([\'homework_assignments\']);\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction openableAssignmentId(item: ClassroomItem): string | null {\\r\\n  if (!item.source_id) return null;\\r\\n  if (item.source_table && !ASSIGNMENT_SOURCE_TABLES.has(item.source_table)) return null;\\r\\n  return item.source_id;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Resolve a classroom_items row to the right student-facing route.\\r\\n * Returns the path to navigate to, or null if the item is not actionable.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function resolveItemRoute(item: ClassroomItem): string | null {\\r\\n  switch (item.type) {\\r\\n    case \'drill\':\\r\\n      if (item.source_table === \'guided_drill_assignments\' && item.source_id) {\\r\\n        return `/guided/${encodeURIComponent(item.source_id)}?returnTo=${encodeURIComponent(\'/classroom\')}`;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (item.source_id) return `/drill?assignmentId=${encodeURIComponent(item.source_id)}`;\\r\\n      return \'/drill\';\\r\\n    case \'homework\': {\\r\\n      const assignmentId = openableAssignmentId(item);\\r\\n      if (assignmentId) return `/classroom/${encodeURIComponent(assignmentId)}`;\\r\\n      if (isSelfContainedItem(item)) return `/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(item.id)}`;\\r\\n      return \'/classroom\';\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    case \'activity\':\\r\\n      // source_id is the activity_assignments row id; source_table=\'activity_assignments\'.\\r\\n      if (item.source_id) return `/activities/${encodeURIComponent(item.source_id)}`;\\r\\n      return \'/activities\';\\r\\n    case \'practice_test\':\\r\\n      // An assigned PrepTest sitting. The Testing Centre lists exactly the\\r\\n      // assigned PrepTests for a student without the Practice tests switch.\\r\\n      return \'/testing\';\\r\\n    case \'bootcamp_module\':\\r\\n      if (item.source_id) return item.source_id; // expected to be a /bootcamp/* path\\r\\n      return \'/bootcamps\';\\r\\n    case \'session_recap\':\\r\\n    case \'smartboard_recap\':\\r\\n    case \'announcement\':\\r\\n    case \'note\':\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

A correctly typed mirror cannot cause this when its Activity source loaded: `buildAssignedRows` explicitly preserves `activityWorkStatus` over mirror status.

Check these fixture fields:

- `activity_assignments.completed_at`: `activityWorkStatus` requires both `status='completed'` and a timestamp. A null timestamp downgrades completed work to Assigned without saved-response evidence.
- `classroom_items.source_table`: null or legacy values create a separate Assigned mirror even when its source Activity appears in Completed.
- Whether the Activity summary request failed or remains pending, leaving only mirror evidence.

Smallest fixes:

- Null completion timestamp: change Activity-only completion projection in `src/lib/studentWorkProjection.ts:67`; add missing-timestamp cases to its tests and `useAssignedTodoFeed.test.ts`.
- Legacy mirror identity: canonicalize to `activity_assignments:<id>` only when a fetched Activity assignment proves the ID match. Add completed-source/stale-mirror and repeat-assignment tests in `useAssignedTodoFeed.test.ts`.

No edits made. Parent has been asked to inspect the missing fixture fields before choosing the fix.

return {\\r\\n      ...drill,\\r\\n      bank_filter: local.bank_filter,\\r\\n      question_count: drill.question_count ?? local.question_count,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return drill;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface GuidedDrillStartProps {\\r\\n  /** When embedded (not routed), the drill slug to render. Falls back to the URL param. */\\r\\n  assignmentId?: string;\\r\\n  /** When embedded, called instead of navigating away on return. */\\r\\n  onReturn?: () => void;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function GuidedDrillStart({\\r\\n  slug: slugProp,\\r\\n  assignmentId: assignmentIdProp,\\r\\n  onReturn,\\r\\n}: GuidedDrillStartProps = {}) {\\r\\n  const params = useParams<{ assignmentId?: string; slug?: string }>();\\r\\n  const assignmentId = assignmentIdProp ?? params.assignmentId;\\r\\n  const slug = slugProp ?? params.slug;\\r\\n  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();\\r\\n  const requestedRunId = searchParams.get(\'run\');\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const location = useLocation();\\r\\n  const { isLoading: qbLoading } = useQuestionBank();\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n\\r\\n  const stateBootcampOverride = (location.state as any)?.bootcampOverride as PracticePool | undefined;\\r\\n  const slugBootcampOverride = slug ? getPracticePool(slug) : undefined;\\r\\n  const bootcampOverride: PracticePool | undefined = stateBootcampOverride ?? slugBootcampOverride;\\r\\n  const originReturnTo = safeGuidedReturnPath(\\r\\n    searchParams.get(\'returnTo\'),\\r\\n    GUIDED_DRILLS_HOME_RETURN_TO,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  const resumeQids = React.useMemo(\\r\\n    () => parseGuidedResumeQids(searchParams.get(\'resumeQids\')),\\r\\n    [searchParams],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  const resumeIndex = React.useMemo(\\r\\n    () => parseGuidedResumeIndex(searchParams.get(\'resumeIndex\'), resumeQids),\\r\\n    [searchParams, resumeQids],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  const launchReturnTo = React.useMemo(\\r\\n    () => buildGuidedLaunchReturnPath({\\r\\n      assignmentId,\\r\\n      slug,\\r\\n      originReturnTo,\\r\\n    }),\\r\\n    [assignmentId, slug, originReturnTo],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  const [resolvedGuidedDrillId, setResolvedGuidedDrillId] = React.useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Keys this drill\'s durable resume row. Assignment id when there is one, so a\\r\\n  // re-assignment of the same drill later gets its own clean run.\\r\\n  const guidedKey = React.useMemo(\\r\\n    () => guidedSessionKey({ assignmentId, drillId: resolvedGuidedDrillId }),\\r\\n    [assignmentId, resolvedGuidedDrillId],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  const guidedLegacyKey = React.useMemo(\\r\\n    () =>\\r\\n      resolvedGuidedDrillId && !assignmentId\\r\\n        ? guidedSessionKey({ slug })\\r\\n        : null,\\r\\n    [resolvedGuidedDrillId, assignmentId, slug],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  // An unfinished guided run is loaded before Start so its frozen question queue\\r\\n  // can be reused. Without this the level draw runs again on re-entry and\\r\\n  // reshuffles the pool, which is what makes a guided drill look like it\\r\\n  // \\"restarted\\" — the student gets different questions, not just question 1.\\r\\n  const [storedGuided, setStoredGuided] = React.useState<GuidedDrillSession | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [guidedRuns, setGuidedRuns] = React.useState<StandaloneDrillRun[]>([]);\\r\\n  const [runsLoading, setRunsLoading] = React.useState(true);\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    let active = true;\\r\\n    setGuidedRuns([]);\\r\\n    setRunsLoading(true);\\r\\n    if (!user || !guidedKey || bootcampOverride) { setRunsLoading(false); return; }\\r\\n    void listStandaloneDrillRuns(user.id).then((runs) => {\\r\\n      if (active) setGuidedRuns(runs.filter((run) => run.source_key === guidedKey));\\r\\n    }).catch(() => toast.error(\\"Could not load your guided drill sittings. Please retry.\\")).finally(() => {\\r\\n      if (active) setRunsLoading(false);\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    return () => { active = false; };\\r\\n  }, [user?.id, guidedKey, bootcampOverride]);\\r\\n  const activeGuidedRun = React.useMemo(() =>\\r\\n    guidedRuns.find((run) => run.session.status === \'in_progress\' && run.session.attempts.length > 0) ?? null,\\r\\n  [guidedRuns]);\\r\\n  const completedGuidedRuns = React.useMemo(() =>\\r\\n    guidedRuns.filter((run) => run.session.status === \'completed\' && run.session.attempts.length > 0),\\r\\n  [guidedRuns]);\\r\\n  const visibleStoredGuided = isLegacyGuidedSessionSuperseded(\\r\\n    storedGuided, completedGuidedRuns.map((run) => run.session),\\r\\n  ) ? null : storedGuided;\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    let cancelled = false;\\r\\n    setStoredGuided(null);\\r\\n    if (!user || !guidedKey || bootcampOverride) return;\\r\\n    void loadGuidedDrillSession(user.id, guidedKey, guidedLegacyKey).then((stored) => {\\r\\n      if (cancelled) return;\\r\\n      setStoredGuided(stored && stored.status === \'in_progress\' && stored.attempts.length > 0 ? stored : null);\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      cancelled = true;\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }, [user, guidedKey, guidedLegacyKey, bootcampOverride]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // A finished run is shown back to the student instead of being silently\\r\\n  // redrawn. Completion deliberately makes a row unresumable so a re-attempt is\\r\\n  // fresh, but that also meant reopening a completed drill reshuffled the pool\\r\\n  // and left no way to see the questions they had just answered — the work was\\r\\n  // still in the store, just never read. `retaking` is the escape hatch: once\\r\\n  // they ask for a fresh attempt we stop showing the review for this visit.\\r\\n  const [completedGuided, setCompletedGuided] = React.useState<GuidedDrillSession | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [retaking, setRetaking] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  const [showLegacyResults, setShowLegacyResults] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    let cancelled = false;\\r\\n    setCompletedGuided(null);\\r\\n    setRetaking(false);\\r\\n    if (!user || !guidedKey || bootcampOverride) return;\\r\\n    void loadCompletedGuidedDrillSession(user.id, guidedKey, guidedLegacyKey).then((stored) => {\\r\\n      if (!cancelled) setCompletedGuided(stored);\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      cancelled = true;\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }, [user, guidedKey, guidedLegacyKey, bootcampOverride]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    void loadPracticeSession(user.id, bootcampOverride.slug).then((stored) => {\\r\\n      if (cancelled) return;\\r\\n      if (!stored || stored.status !== \'in_progress\' ||  );\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      cancelled = true;\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }, [user, bootcampOverride, visibleStoredGuided, activeGuidedRun]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const [assignment, setAssignment] = React.useState<AssignmentWithDrill | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [directDrill, setDirectDrill] = React.useState<GuidedDrill | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    setLoading(true);\\r\\n    setAssignment(null);\\r\\n    setDirectDrill(null);\\r\\n    setResolvedGuidedDrillId(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n    if (assignmentId) {\\r\\n      getAssignment(assignmentId).then((a) => {\\r\\n        setAssignment(a as AssignmentWithDrill | null);\\r\\n        setResolvedGuidedDrillId((a as AssignmentWithDrill to the bundled definition when the DB row is missing, and\\r\\n        // backfill an empty/absent bank_filter from the local seed so the pool\\r\\n        // can still resolve client-side. Please refresh and try again.\\");\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (qbLoading) {\\r\\n      toast.info(\'Loading question bank...\');\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Intro to LR practice drills — use prebuilt MAIN_CONCLUSION_PRACTICE_POOLS qids.\\r\\n    if (bootcampOverride && bootcampOverride.source === \'intro-to-lr-practice-drills\') {\\r\\n      const ov = bootcampOverride;\\r\\n      const feature = ov.qfeatures[0];\\r\\n\\r\\n      const poolQids = resumeQids.length > 0 ? resumeQids : ov.qids;\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (!Array.isArray(poolQids) || poolQids.length === 0) {\\r\\n        toast.error(\\r\\n          `Practice drill pool is missing or empty for ${ov.slug}. Expected qtype = Main Conclusion, feature = ${feature}, count = ${ov.count}.`,\\r\\n        );\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      const known = new Set(questionBank.getAllQuestions().map((q) => q.qid));\\r\\n      const validQids = poolQids.filter((qid) => known.has(qid));\\r\\n      const invalidQids = poolQids.filter((qid) => !known.has(qid));\\r\\n\\r\\n      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console\\r\\n      console.log(\'[intro-to-lr-practice-drills] launch\', {\\r\\n        slug: ov.slug,\\r\\n        title: ov.title,\\r\\n        qtypes: ov.qtypes,\\r\\n        qfeatures: ov.qfeatures,\\r\\n        difficulties: ov.difficulties,\\r\\n        count: ov.count,\\r\\n        poolQids,\\r\\n        validQids,\\r\\n        invalidQids,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (invalidQids.length > 0) {\\r\\n        toast.error(\'Practice drill pool contains invalid qids. Check practiceDrillPools.ts.\');\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (assignment) {\\r\\n        await markCardRead(assignment.id);\\r\\n        await markStarted(assignment.id);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Named by the pool slug rather than by a copy of its questions. The\\r\\n      // slug is also what lets /drill mark this practice set complete in module\\r\\n      // progress when the student finishes (see runner path-completion), and\\r\\n      // what its Run Record is keyed by, so a refresh finds the same run.\\r\\n      navigate(\\r\\n        `/drill?${launchDrill({\\r\\n          kind: \'practice-set\',\\r\\n          slug: ov.slug,\\r\\n          source: ov.source,\\r\\n          options: {\\r\\n            returnTo: launchReturnTo,\\r\\n            label: ov.title,\\r\\n            initialIndex: resumeIndex ?? undefined,\\r\\n          },\\r\\n        })}`,\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    if (assignment) {\\r\\n      await markCardRead(assignment.id);\\r\\n      await markStarted(assignment.id);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (!drill) {\\r\\n      toast.error(\\"Couldn\'t build this drill. Please refresh and try again.\\");\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    const resumeRun = requestedRunId\\r\\n      ? guidedRuns.find((run) => run.id === requestedRunId && run.session.status === \'in_progress\')\\r\\n      : activeGuidedRun;\\r\\n    if (requestedRunId && !resumeRun) {\\r\\n      toast.error(\'This saved guided drill could not be found in your account.\');\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const launch = launchDrill({\\r\\n        kind: \'guided-drill\',\\r\\n        drillId: drill.id,\\r\\n        assignmentId: assignment?.id,\\r\\n        durationMinutes: drill.estimated_minutes ?? undefined,\\r\\n        options: {\\r\\n          returnTo: launchReturnTo,\\r\\n          label: drill.title,\\r\\n          initialIndex: resumeIndex ?? undefined,\\r\\n        },\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    navigate(`/drill?${launch}${resumeRun ? `&run=${encodeURIComponent(resumeRun.id)}` : \'\'}`);\\r\\n  }, [assignment, drill, navigate, qbLoading, bootcampOverride, slug, resumeQids, resumeIndex, storedGuided, launchReturnTo, requestedRunId, guidedRuns, activeGuidedRun]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (loading) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background text-foreground p-8\\">Loading...</div>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!drill && !bootcampOverride) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background text-foreground p-8\\">Guided drill not found.</div>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Reopening a drill they already finished: show what they did. The question\\r\\n  // bank has to be loaded first, since the scorecard resolves each qid through\\r\\n  // it — without that gate the rows render blank for a moment.\\r\\n  if (runsLoading && !bootcampOverride) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background text-foreground p-8\\" role=\\"status\\">Loading your guided drill sittings…</div>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if ((completedGuidedRuns.length > 0 || legacyCompletedVisible) && !retaking && !activeGuidedRun && !visibleStoredGuided && !qbLoading) {\\r\\n    if (showLegacyResults && legacyCompletedVisible) return (\\r\\n      <PracticeSetResults\\r\\n        session={completedSession as DrillSession}\\r\\n        title={drill?.title ?? \'Your results\'}\\r\\n        subtitle=\\"Earlier guided drill sitting\\"\\r\\n        onBack={() => setShowLegacyResults(false)}\\r\\n        onRetry={() => { setRetaking(true); void handleStart(); }}\\r\\n      />\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    if (completedGuidedRuns.length > 0) return (\\r\\n      <main className=\\"mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-5 px-5 py-8\\">\\r\\n        <Button variant=\\"ghost\\" onClick={handleReturn}>Back</Button>\\r\\n        <h1 className=\\"text-2xl font-semibold\\">{drill?.title ?? \'Guided drill\'}</h1>\\r\\n        <p className=\\"text-muted-foreground\\">Each completed sitting is saved separately. Open any one to see its results and answers.</p>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"space-y-3\\">{completedGuidedRuns.map((run) => (\\r\\n          <div key={run.id} className=\\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4\\">\\r\\n            <div><strong>{new Date(run.updated_at).toLocaleDateString()}</strong><p className=\\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">{run.session.attempts.length} answered</p></div>\\r\\n            <Button variant=\\"outline\\" onClick={() => navigate(`/practice/drills/runs/${run.id}?view=results`)}>Results & review</Button>\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n        ))}{legacyCompletedVisible && <div className=\\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4\\">\\r\\n          <div><strong>Earlier sitting</strong><p className=\\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">Saved before individual sittings were available</p></div>\\r\\n          <Button variant=\\"outline\\" onClick={() => setShowLegacyResults(true)}>Results & review…193 tokens truncated…g a link, and a teacher route would\\r\\n * bounce them to auth.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport const GUIDED_DRILLS_HOME_RETURN_TO = \'/classroom\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function safeGuidedReturnPath(value: string | null | undefined, fallback: string): string {\\r\\n  if (!value || !value.startsWith(\'/\') || value.startsWith(\'//\')) return fallback;\\r\\n  return value;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function parseGuidedResumeQids(value: string | null | undefined): string[] {\\r\\n  return (value ?? \'\')\\r\\n    .split(\',\')\\r\\n    .map((qid) => qid.trim())\\r\\n    .filter(Boolean);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function parseGuidedResumeIndex(value: string | null | undefined, qids: string[]): number | null {\\r\\n  if (qids.length === 0 || !value) return null;\\r\\n  const parsed = Number.parseInt(value, 10);\\r\\n  if (!Number.isFinite(parsed)) return null;\\r\\n  return Math.min(Math.max(parsed, 0), qids.length - 1);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function buildGuidedLaunchReturnPath(input: {\\r\\n  assignmentId?: string | null;\\r\\n  slug?: string | null;\\r\\n  originReturnTo: string;\\r\\n  resumeQids?: string[];\\r\\n  resumeIndex?: number;\\r\\n}): string {\\r\\n  const base = input.assignmentId\\r\\n    ? `/guided/${encodeURIComponent(input.assignmentId)}`\\r\\n    : input.slug\\r\\n      ? `/guided-drills/${encodeURIComponent(input.slug)}`\\r\\n      : \'/classroom\';\\r\\n\\r\\n  const params = new URLSearchParams();\\r\\n  params.set(\'returnTo\', input.originReturnTo);\\r\\n  if (input.resumeQids?.length) {\\r\\n    params.set(\'resumeQids\', input.resumeQids.join(\',\'));\\r\\n    params.set(\'resumeIndex\', String(input.resumeIndex ?? 0));\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return `${base}?${params.toString()}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function appendGuidedResumeParams(\\r\\n  launchReturnTo: string,\\r\\n  qids: string[],\\r\\n  currentIndex: number,\\r\\n): string {\\r\\n  if (!launchReturnTo.startsWith(\'/guided\')) return launchReturnTo;\\r\\n  const [path, query = \'\'] = launchReturnTo.split(\'?\');\\r\\n  const params = new URLSearchParams(query);\\r\\n  if (qids.length > 0) {\\r\\n    params.set(\'resumeQids\', qids.join(\',\'));\\r\\n    params.set(\'resumeIndex\', String(Math.min(Math.max(currentIndex, 0), qids.length - 1)));\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const next = params.toString();\\r\\n  return next ? `${path}?${next}` : path;\\r\\n}\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Turning a Drill Launch\'s *name* into the questions it stands for.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A launch names a run rather than containing it, so the kinds named by a row\\r\\n * have to read that row before the runner can start. This is the read.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A row that is gone is a dead link, and a dead link says so. It does not fall\\r\\n * back to a generic drill: a student quietly drilling something nobody assigned\\r\\n * is worse than a student telling you a link is broken. See\\r\\n * `docs/adr/0016-a-drill-link-names-a-run-it-does-not-contain-one.md`.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { DrillLaunchError, launchNeedsResolution, type DrillLaunch } from \'@/lib/drillLaunch\';\\r\\nimport { listSavedSets } from \'@/lib/savedSets\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { getPracticePool } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/data/practiceDrillPools\';\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  pickHomeworkForDay,\\r\\n  pickPracticeForDay,\\r\\n} from \'@/bootcamps/principle-flaw/lib/dayQuestions\';\\r\\nimport ## assistant

Normal resume is correct without a `runId` in My Work’s link:

- `GuidedDrillStart.tsx:148` finds the signed-in account’s sittings by exact assignment key.
- It selects the newest answered unfinished sitting, shows its resume notice, and passes `run=<id>` into Drill.
- `Drill.tsx:854` validates ownership/source and restores its saved queue, index, and answers.
- Completed sittings expose individual Results & review links.

No resume regression from the evidence adapter.

One existing gap: `DrillLaunchGate.tsx:130` resolves current assignment content before Drill restores the durable sitting. `drillLaunchResolve.ts` checks only legacy resume storage. If the current source pool becomes empty while the saved queue remains valid, Continue fails before restoration.

Missing tests: assignment link with ledger-only unfinished progress; exact saved queue restoration; completed-sitting Results links; valid saved queue with an empty current source pool.

Historical contradictory completion rows remain unchanged.
